# CI runs

GitHub Actions were not dispatched by hand. Pushes to `GUD-Android-Redevelopment` start the workflows whose `on.push` filters match.

The AAB workflow file was invalid. GitHub created a failed run with zero jobs on every push, including commits that are not on `GUD-Android`. Run `38909206998` is that failure on `7ac5e95`. The file was corrected in `9906463`. That commit did not start an AAB run, which is what the `GUD-Android` branch filter is supposed to do. The AAB job itself was not executed.

Kosha CI on `9906463` completed successfully. It runs the legacy Node lint, build, and tests. It does not build `android-native/`.

## 2026-10-17 — M24 source-grounded legacy KOSHA1 omitted metadata now compatible; 25 JVM + 14 Android PASS

**Source audit:** frozen `src/lib/backup.ts@af96dcc8967ab595e1d9acee78e29e500cb99a9e`, `unpackLegacyJson`, supports `KOSHA1` encrypted JSON envelope WITHOUT `kdf` or `iterations` fields; uses PBKDF2-SHA256 and `blob.iterations || PBKDF2_ITERATIONS` (600,000). Native `LegacyBackupEnvelopeReader.decodeKOSHA1` had required explicit `kdf=="PBKDF2-SHA256"` and exactly `600000`, and native `LockedRestoreStaging` used `getString("kdf")` / `getInt("iterations")` in all three decode paths, incorrectly rejecting historical encrypted archives. Corrected native crypto policy accepts empty KDF indicator and zero/missing iteration metadata (ONLY this source-proven implicit default); still rejects unsupported explicit algorithm or nonzero rounds !=600000, relies on GCM AEAD authentication before returning plaintext. All three native staged-reader operations now parse optional metadata consistently. **Does not invent missing inner payload fields or authorize a v1 schema-to-v9 financial migration.**

An extra value in a formerly omitted field (for example an altered `entity.is_active`, `entity.pan_masked`, `account.base_currency`, or a non-null field normally defaulting to NULL) could pass both prepublication verification and read-only crash forensics despite financial/identity data changing. Reworked `verifyExactRows` to read the full allow-listed physical SQLCipher row and the actual PRAGMA table_info schema, assemble the exact expected full value for every field from authenticated planned values plus frozen SQL defaults, then compare typed row multisets. Unexpected, coerced, omitted, added or nondefault values reject publication. Only literal NULL, quoted TEXT, or signed integer defaults are supported; unknown expressions fail closed. The existing `ApprovedNewVaultRestore` prepublication and `RestoreRecoveryInspector` after-crash checks both reuse this unchanged verifier path.

## 2026-10-21 — Strict APK nested Chaquopy binary verification correction
